          <p>This section applies to all policies of accident and health insurance, health maintenance contracts regulated under chapter 62D, health benefit certificates offered through a fraternal benefit society regulated under chapter 64B, and group subscriber contracts offered by nonprofit health service plan corporations regulated under chapter 62C, but does not apply to policies designed primarily to provide coverage payable on a per diem, fixed indemnity or nonexpense incurred basis, or policies that provide only accident coverage.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.26.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Required coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>Every policy, plan, certificate, or contract referred to in subdivision 1 issued or renewed after August 1, 1985, must provide coverage for special dietary treatment for phenylketonuria when recommended by a physician.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.26.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/0/49/#laws.0.41.0">1985 c 49 s 41</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/9/#laws.2.1.0">1Sp1985 c 9 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/564/#laws.1.54.0">1992 c 564 art 1 s 54</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.265">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.265 COVERAGE FOR LYME DISEASE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.265.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Required coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>Every health plan, including a plan providing the coverage specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.011#stat.62A.011.3">62A.011, subdivision 3</a>, clause (10), must cover treatment for diagnosed Lyme disease.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.265.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Special restrictions prohibited.</span></h2>
          <p>No health plan included in subdivision 1 may impose a special deductible, co-payment, waiting period, or other special restriction on treatment for Lyme disease that the health plan does not apply to nonpreventive treatment in general.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.265.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/465/#laws.5.2.0">1996 c 465 art 5 s 2</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.27">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.27 COVERAGE OF ADOPTED CHILDREN.</h1>
        <p>(a) A health plan that provides coverage to a Minnesota resident must cover adopted children of the insured, subscriber, participant, or enrollee on the same basis as other dependents. Consequently, the plan shall not contain any provision concerning preexisting condition limitations, insurability, eligibility, or health underwriting approval concerning children placed for adoption with the participant.</p>
        <p>(b) The coverage required by this section is effective from the date of placement for adoption. For purposes of this section, placement for adoption means the assumption and retention by a person of a legal obligation for total or partial support of a child in anticipation of adoption of the child. The child's placement with a person terminates upon the termination of the legal obligation for total or partial support.</p>
        <p>(c) For the purpose of this section, health plan includes:</p>
        <p>(1) coverage offered by community integrated service networks;</p>
        <p>(2) coverage that is designed solely to provide dental or vision care; and</p>
        <p>(3) any plan under the federal Employee Retirement Income Security Act of 1974 (ERISA), United States Code, title 29, sections 1001 to 1461.</p>
        <p>(d) No policy or contract covered by this section may require notification to a health carrier as a condition for this dependent coverage. However, if the policy or contract mandates an additional premium for each dependent, the health carrier is entitled to all premiums that would have been collected had the health carrier been aware of the additional dependent. The health carrier may withhold payment of any health benefits for the new dependent until it has been compensated with the applicable premium which would have been owed if the health carrier had been informed of the additional dependent immediately.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.27.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/56/#laws.0.1.0">1983 c 56 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/0/49/#laws.0.41.0">1985 c 49 s 41</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/337/#laws.0.53.0">1987 c 337 s 53</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/656/#laws.0.4.0">1988 c 656 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/207/#laws.10.4.0">1995 c 207 art 10 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/225/#laws.2.62.0">1997 c 225 art 2 s 62</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/2/#laws.1.7.0">2003 c 2 art 1 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/255/#laws.0.11.0">2006 c 255 s 11</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.28">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.28 COVERAGE FOR SCALP HAIR PROSTHESES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.28.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope of coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to all policies of accident and health insurance, health maintenance contracts regulated under chapter 62D, health benefit certificates offered through a fraternal benefit society regulated under chapter 64B, and group subscriber contracts offered by nonprofit health service plan corporations regulated under chapter 62C. This section does not apply to policies designed primarily to provide coverage payable on a per diem, fixed indemnity or nonexpense incurred basis, or policies that provide only accident coverage.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.28.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Required coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Every policy, plan, certificate, or contract referred to in subdivision 1 must provide coverage for scalp hair prostheses, including all equipment and accessories necessary for regular use of scalp hair prostheses, worn for hair loss suffered as a result of a health condition, including but not limited to alopecia areata or the treatment for cancer, unless there is a clinical basis for limitation.</p>
          <p>(b) The coverage required by this section is subject to the co-payment, coinsurance, deductible, and other enrollee cost-sharing requirements that apply to similar types of items under the policy, plan, certificate, or contract and may be limited to one prosthesis per benefit year.</p>
          <p>(c) The coverage required by this section for scalp hair prostheses is limited to $1,000 per benefit year.</p>
          <p>(d) A scalp hair prosthesis must be prescribed by a doctor to be covered under this section.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.28.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/202/#laws.0.1.0">1987 c 202 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/564/#laws.1.54.0">1992 c 564 art 1 s 54</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.7.1.0">2004 c 288 art 7 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/84/#laws.1.22.0">2013 c 84 art 1 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.57.5.0">2024 c 127 art 57 s 5</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.285">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.285 PROHIBITED UNDERWRITING; BREAST IMPLANTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.285.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope of coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to all policies of accident and health insurance regulated under this chapter, subscriber contracts offered by nonprofit health service plan corporations regulated under chapter 62C, health maintenance contracts regulated under chapter 62D, and health benefit certificates offered through a fraternal benefit society regulated under chapter 64B. This section does not apply to policies, plans, certificates, or contracts payable on a fixed indemnity or non-expense-incurred basis, or policies, plans, certificates, or contracts that provide only accident coverage.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.285.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Required coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>No policy, plan, certificate, or contract referred to in subdivision 1 shall be issued or renewed to provide coverage to a Minnesota resident if it provides an exclusion, reduction, or other limitation as to coverage, deductible, coinsurance, or co-payment applicable solely to conditions caused by breast implants.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.285.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Refusal to issue or renew.</span></h2>
          <p>No issuer of a policy, plan, certificate, or contract referred to in subdivision 1 shall refuse to issue or renew at standard premium rates a policy, plan, certificate, or contract referred to in subdivision 1 solely because the prospective insured or enrollee has breast implants.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.285.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/483/#laws.0.55.0">2000 c 483 s 55</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.285.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/564/#laws.4.8.0">1992 c 564 art 4 s 8</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.29">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.29 SURETY BOND OR SECURITY FOR CERTAIN HEALTH BENEFIT PLANS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.29.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Surety bond or security requirement.</span></h2>
          <p>Any employer, except the state and its political subdivisions as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/65B.43#stat.65B.43.20">65B.43, subdivision 20</a>, who provides a health benefit plan to its Minnesota employees, which is to some extent self-insured by the employer, and who purchases stop-loss insurance coverage, or any other insurance coverage, in connection with the health benefit plan, shall annually file with the commissioner, within 60 days of the end of the employer's fiscal year, security acceptable to the commissioner in an amount specified under subdivision 2, or a surety bond in the form and amount prescribed by subdivisions 2 and 3. An acceptable surety bond is one issued by a corporate surety authorized by the commissioner to transact this business in the state of Minnesota for the purposes of this section. The term "Minnesota employees" includes any Minnesota resident who is employed by the employer.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.29.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Amount of surety bond or security.</span></h2>
          <p>The amount of surety bond or acceptable security required by subdivision 1 shall be equal to one-fourth of the projected annual medical and hospital expenses to be incurred by the employer or $1,000, whichever is greater, with respect to its Minnesota employees by reason of the portion of the employer's health benefit plan which is self-insured by the employer.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.29.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Form of the surety bond.</span></h2>
          <p>The surety bond shall provide as follows:</p>
          <p class="ce">SURETY BOND</p>
          <p>KNOW ALL PERSONS BY THESE PRESENTS: That (entity to be bonded), of (location), (hereinafter called the "principal"), as principal, and (bonding company name), a (name of state) corporation, of (location) (hereinafter called the "surety"), as surety are held and firmly bound unto the commissioner of commerce of the state of Minnesota for the use and benefit of Minnesota residents entitled to health benefits from the principal in the sum of ($.........), for the payment of which well and truly to be made, the principal binds itself, its successor and assigns, and the surety binds itself and its successors and assigns, jointly and severally, firmly by these presents.</p>
          <p>WHEREAS, in accordance with section (......) of the Minnesota Statute, principal is required to file a surety bond with the commissioner of commerce of the state of Minnesota.</p>
          <p>NOW, THEREFORE, the condition of this obligation is such that if the said principal shall, according to the terms, provisions, and limitations of principals' health benefit program for its Minnesota employees, pay all of its liabilities and obligations, including all benefits as provided in the attached plan, then, this obligation shall be null and void, otherwise to remain in full force and effect, subject, however, to the following terms and conditions:</p>
          <p>1. The liability of the surety is limited to the payment of the benefits of the employee benefit plan which are payable by the principal and within the amount of the bond. The surety shall be bound to payments owed by the principal for obligations arising from a default of the principal or any loss incurred during the period to which the bond applies.</p>
          <p>2. In the event of any default on the part of the principal to abide by the terms and provision of the attached plan, the commissioner of commerce may, upon ten-days notice to the surety and opportunity to be heard, require the surety to pay all of the principal's past and future obligations under the attached plan with respect to the principal's Minnesota employees.</p>
          <p>3. Service on the surety shall be deemed to be service on the principals.</p>
          <p>4. This bond shall be in effect from ............... to ................, and may not be canceled by either the surety or the principal.</p>
          <p>5. Any Minnesota employee of principal aggrieved by a default of principal under the attached plan, and/or the commissioner of commerce on behalf of any such employee, may enforce the provisions of this bond.</p>
          <p>6. This bond shall become effective at (time of day, month, day, year).</p>
          <p>IN TESTIMONY WHEREOF, said principals and said surety have caused this instrument to be signed by their respective, duly authorized officers and their corporate seals to be hereunto affixed this (day, month, year).</p>
          <table>
            <col style="width:38.4615384615385%">
            <col style="width:23.0769230769231%">
            <col style="width:38.4615384615385%">
            <tr>
              <td>Signed, sealed and delivered in the presence of:</td>
              <td></td>
              <td>Corporation Name</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>
                <span class="leader">.</span>
              </td>
              <td></td>
              <td>
                <span class="leader">.</span>
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>
                <span class="leader">.</span>
              </td>
              <td></td>
              <td>
                <span class="leader">.</span>
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>
                <span class="leader">.</span>
              </td>
              <td></td>
              <td>Bonding Company Name</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>
                <span class="leader">.</span>
              </td>
              <td></td>
              <td>
                <span>By:</span>
                <span class="leader">.</span>
              </td>
            </tr>
          </table>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.29.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Penalty for failure to comply.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of revenue shall deny any business tax deduction to an employer for the employer's contribution to a health plan for the period which the employer fails to comply with this section. This section does not apply to trusts established under chapter 62H which have been approved by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.29.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Petition to reduce bond or security amount.</span></h2>
          <p>An employer subject to this section may petition the commissioner to, and the commissioner may, allow the use of a surety bond not in the form specified in subdivision 3, or grant a reduction in the amount of the surety bond or security required.</p>
          <p>In reviewing a petition submitted under this subdivision, the commissioner must consider, in addition to any other factors, information provided by the petitioner in regard to the following:</p>
          <p>(1) the size of the petitioner's business;</p>
          <p>(2) the number of employees;</p>
          <p>(3) the cost of providing the bond or security and the effect the cost will have on the petitioner's financial condition;</p>
          <p>(4) whether the cost of the bond or security will impair the petitioner's ability to self-insure; and</p>
          <p>(5) the petitioner's likelihood of being able to meet the petitioner's future obligations in regard to the health plan.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.29.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/337/#laws.0.54.0">1987 c 337 s 54</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.30">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.30 COVERAGE FOR DIAGNOSTIC PROCEDURES FOR CANCER.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.30.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope of coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to all policies of accident and health insurance, health maintenance contracts regulated under chapter 62D, health benefit certificates offered through a fraternal benefit society regulated under chapter 64B, and subscriber contracts offered by nonprofit health service plan corporations regulated under chapter 62C, but does not apply to policies designed primarily to provide coverage payable on a per diem, fixed indemnity or nonexpense incurred basis, or policies that provide only accident coverage.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.30.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Required coverage.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Every policy, plan, certificate, or contract referred to in subdivision 1 that provides coverage to a Minnesota resident must provide coverage for routine screening procedures for cancer and the office or facility visit, including mammograms, surveillance tests for ovarian cancer for women who are at risk for ovarian cancer as defined in subdivision 3, pap smears, and colorectal screening tests for men and women, when ordered or provided by a physician in accordance with the standard practice of medicine.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.30.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Ovarian cancer surveillance tests.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of subdivision 2:</p>
          <p>(a) "At risk for ovarian cancer" means:</p>
          <p>(1) having a family history:</p>
          <p>(i) with one or more first- or second-degree relatives with ovarian cancer;</p>
          <p>(ii) of clusters of women relatives with breast cancer; or</p>
          <p>(iii) of nonpolyposis colorectal cancer; or</p>
          <p>(2) testing positive for BRCA1 or BRCA2 mutations.</p>
          <p>(b) "Surveillance tests for ovarian cancer" means annual screening using:</p>
          <p>(1) CA-125 serum tumor marker testing;</p>
          <p>(2) transvaginal ultrasound;</p>
          <p>(3) pelvic examination; or</p>
          <p>(4) other proven ovarian cancer screening tests currently being evaluated by the federal Food and Drug Administration or by the National Cancer Institute.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.30.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Mammograms.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of subdivision 2, coverage for a preventive mammogram screening (1) includes digital breast tomosynthesis for enrollees at risk for breast cancer, and (2) is covered as a preventive item or service, as described under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62Q.46">62Q.46</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) For purposes of this subdivision, "digital breast tomosynthesis" means a radiologic procedure that involves the acquisition of projection images over the stationary breast to produce cross-sectional digital three-dimensional images of the breast. "At risk for breast cancer" means:</p>
          <p>(1) having a family history with one or more first- or second-degree relatives with breast cancer;</p>
          <p>(2) testing positive for BRCA1 or BRCA2 mutations;</p>
          <p>(3) having heterogeneously dense breasts or extremely dense breasts based on the Breast Imaging Reporting and Data System established by the American College of Radiology; or</p>
          <p>(4) having a previous diagnosis of breast cancer.</p>
          <p>(c) This subdivision does not apply to coverage provided through a public health care program under chapter 256B or 256L.</p>
          <p>(d) Nothing in this subdivision limits the coverage of digital breast tomosynthesis in a policy, plan, certificate, or contract referred to in subdivision 1 that is in effect prior to January 1, 2020.</p>
          <p>(e) Nothing in this subdivision prohibits a policy, plan, certificate, or contract referred to in subdivision 1 from covering digital breast tomosynthesis for an enrollee who is not at risk for breast cancer.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.30.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Mammogram; diagnostic services and testing.</span></h2>
          <p>If a health care provider determines an enrollee requires additional diagnostic services or testing after a mammogram, a health plan must provide coverage for the additional diagnostic services or testing with no cost-sharing, including co-pay, deductible, or coinsurance.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.30.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Application.</span></h2>
          <p>If the application of subdivision 5 before an enrollee has met their health plan's deductible would result in: (1) health savings account ineligibility under United States Code, title 26, section 223; or (2) catastrophic health plan ineligibility under United States Code, title 42, section 18022(e), then subdivision 5 shall apply to diagnostic services or testing only after the enrollee has met their health plan's deductible.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.30.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/441/#laws.0.2.0">1988 c 441 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/642/#laws.0.5.0">1988 c 642 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/564/#laws.1.32.0">1992 c 564 art 1 s 32</a>,54; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/465/#laws.3.11.0">1994 c 465 art 3 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.6.2.0">2004 c 288 art 6 s 2</a>,3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/66/#laws.0.1.0">2007 c 66 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/344/#laws.0.11.0">2008 c 344 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/9/#laws.8.1.0">1Sp2019 c 9 art 8 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.2.2.0">2023 c 70 art 2 s 2</a>,3</p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.62A.301"><b>62A.301</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/147/#laws.12.16.0">2007 c 147 art 12 s 16</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.302">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.302 COVERAGE OF DEPENDENTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.302.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope of coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to:</p>
          <p>(1) a health plan as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.011">62A.011</a>; and</p>
          <p>(2) a policy, contract, or certificate issued by a community integrated service network licensed under chapter 62N.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.302.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Required coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>Every health plan included in subdivision 1 that provides dependent coverage must define "dependent" no more restrictively than the definition provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62L.02#stat.62L.02.11">62L.02, subdivision 11</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.302.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">No additional restrictions permitted.</span></h2>
          <p>Any health plan included in subdivision 1 that provides dependent coverage of children shall make that coverage available to children until the child attains 26 years of age. A health carrier must not place restrictions on this coverage and must comply with the following requirements:</p>
          <p>(1) with respect to a child who has not attained 26 years of age, a health carrier shall not define dependent for purposes of eligibility for dependent coverage of children other than the terms of a relationship between a child and the enrollee or spouse of the enrollee;</p>
          <p>(2) a health carrier must not deny or restrict coverage for a child who has not attained 26 years of age based on (i) the presence or absence of the child's financial dependency upon the participant, primary subscriber, or any other person; (ii) residency with the participant and in the individual market the primary subscriber, or with any other person; (iii) marital status; (iv) student status; (v) employment; or (vi) any combination of those factors; and</p>
          <p>(3) a health carrier must not deny or restrict coverage of a child based on eligibility for other coverage, except as provided in subdivision 5.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.302.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Grandchildren.</span></h2>
          <p>Nothing in this section requires a health carrier to make coverage available for a grandchild, unless the grandparent becomes the legal guardian or adoptive parent of that grandchild or unless the grandchild meets the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.042">62A.042</a>. For grandchildren included under a grandparent's policy pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.042">62A.042</a>, coverage for the grandchild may terminate if the grandchild does not continue to reside with the covered grandparent continuously from birth, if the grandchild does not remain financially dependent upon the covered grandparent, or when the grandchild reaches age 25, except as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.14">62A.14</a> or if coverage is continued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.20">62A.20</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.302.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Terms of coverage of dependents.</span></h2>
          <p>The terms of coverage in a health plan offered by a health carrier providing dependent coverage of children cannot vary based on age except for children who are 26 years of age or older.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.302.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Opportunity to enroll.</span></h2>
          <p>A health carrier must comply with all provisions of the Affordable Care Act in regards to providing an opportunity to enroll in coverage to any child whose coverage ended, or was not eligible for coverage under a group health plan or individual health plan because, under the terms of the coverage, the availability of dependent coverage of a child ended before age 26.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.302.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Grandfathered plan coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For plan years beginning before January 1, 2014, a group health plan that is a grandfathered plan and makes available dependent coverage of children may exclude an adult child who has not attained 26 years of age from coverage only if the adult child is eligible to enroll in an eligible employer-sponsored health benefit plan, as defined in section 5000A(f)(2) of the Internal Revenue Code, other than the group health plan of a parent.</p>
          <p>(b) For plan years beginning on or after January 1, 2014, a group health plan that is a grandfathered plan must comply with all requirements of this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.302.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Compliance.</span></h2>
          <p>This section does not require compliance with any provision of the Affordable Care Act before the effective date provided for that provision in the Affordable Care Act.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.302.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Enforcement.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall enforce this section.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.302.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/549/#laws.3.9.0">1992 c 549 art 3 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/0/215/#laws.0.12.0">2001 c 215 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/84/#laws.1.23.0">2013 c 84 art 1 s 23</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.3021">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.3021 COVERAGE OF DEPENDENTS BY PLANS OTHER THAN HEALTH PLANS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3021.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope of coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to coverage described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.011#stat.62A.011.3">62A.011, subdivision 3</a>, clauses (4), (6), (7), (8), (9), and (10).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3021.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Dependent.</span></h2>
          <p>"Dependent" means an eligible employee's spouse, unmarried child who is under the age of 25, dependent child of any age who is disabled and who meets the eligibility criteria in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.14#stat.62A.14.2">62A.14, subdivision 2</a>, or any other person whom state or federal law requires to be treated as a dependent for purposes of health plans. For the purpose of this definition, a child includes a child for whom the employee or the employee's spouse has been appointed legal guardian and an adoptive child as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.27">62A.27</a>. A child also includes grandchildren as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.042">62A.042</a> with continued eligibility of grandchildren as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.302#stat.62A.302.4">62A.302, subdivision 4</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.3021.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/84/#laws.1.24.0">2013 c 84 art 1 s 24</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.303">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.303 PROHIBITION; SEVERING OF GROUPS.</h1>
        <p>Section 62L.12, subdivisions 3 and 4, apply to all employer group health plans, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.011">62A.011</a>, regardless of the size of the group.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.303.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/549/#laws.3.10.0">1992 c 549 art 3 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/625/#laws.10.4.0">1994 c 625 art 10 s 4</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.304">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.304 COVERAGE FOR PORT-WINE STAIN ELIMINATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.304.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope of coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to all health plans as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.011">62A.011</a> that provide coverage to a Minnesota resident.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.304.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Required coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>Every health plan included in subdivision 1 must cover elimination or maximum feasible treatment of port-wine stains for any covered person who is a Minnesota resident. No health carrier may reduce or eliminate coverage due to this requirement.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.304.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Rate increases prohibited.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of commerce shall not approve any rate increases due to coverage required under subdivision 2. No health maintenance organization, as defined in chapter 62D, shall increase rates due to coverage required under subdivision 2.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.304.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/116/#laws.0.1.0">1993 c 116 s 1</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.305">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.305 FIBROCYSTIC CONDITION; TERMINATION OR REDUCTION OF COVERAGE.</h1>
        <p>No health plan shall be terminated, canceled, nonrenewed, or contain any increased premium rate, or exclusion, reduction, or limitation on benefits, nor shall coverage be denied, solely because the covered person has been diagnosed as having a fibrocystic breast condition.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.305.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/442/#laws.0.1.0">1994 c 442 s 1</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.306">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.306 USE OF GENDER PROHIBITED.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.306.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Applicability.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to all health plans as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.011">62A.011</a> offered, sold, issued, or renewed, by a health carrier on or after January 1, 1995.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.306.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Prohibition on use of gender.</span></h2>
          <p>No health plan described in subdivision 1 shall determine the premium rate or any other underwriting decision, including initial issuance, through a method that is in any way based upon the gender of any person covered or to be covered under the health plan. This subdivision prohibits use of marital status or generalized differences in expected costs between employees and spouses or between principal insureds and their spouses.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.306.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/625/#laws.10.5.0">1994 c 625 art 10 s 5</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.307">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.307 PRESCRIPTION DRUGS; EQUAL TREATMENT OF PRESCRIBERS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.307.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope of requirement.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to any of the following if issued or renewed to a Minnesota resident or to cover a Minnesota resident:</p>
          <p>(1) a health plan, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.011">62A.011</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) coverage described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.011#stat.62A.011.3">62A.011, subdivision 3</a>, clause (2), (3), or (6) to (12); and</p>
          <p>(3) a policy, contract, or certificate issued by a community integrated service network licensed under chapter 62N.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.307.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Requirement.</span></h2>
          <p>Coverage described in subdivision 1 that covers prescription drugs must provide the same coverage for a prescription written by a health care provider authorized to prescribe the particular drug covered by the health coverage described in subdivision 1, regardless of the type of health care provider that wrote the prescription. This section is intended to prohibit denial of coverage based on the prescription having been written by an advanced practice registered nurse under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148.235">148.235</a>, a physician assistant under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/147A.185">147A.185</a>, or any other nonphysician health care provider authorized to prescribe the particular drug.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.307.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/69/#laws.0.1.0">1995 c 69 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/305/#laws.1.17.0">1996 c 305 art 1 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/225/#laws.2.62.0">1997 c 225 art 2 s 62</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/115/#laws.2.1.0">2020 c 115 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/25/#laws.0.5.0">2023 c 25 s 5</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.3075">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.3075 CANCER CHEMOTHERAPY TREATMENT COVERAGE.</h1>
        <p>(a) A health plan company that provides coverage under a health plan for cancer chemotherapy treatment shall not require a higher co-payment, deductible, or coinsurance amount for a prescribed, orally administered anticancer medication that is used to kill or slow the growth of cancerous cells than what the health plan requires for an intravenously administered or injected cancer medication that is provided, regardless of formulation or benefit category determination by the health plan company.</p>
        <p>(b) A health plan company must not achieve compliance with this section by imposing an increase in co-payment, deductible, or coinsurance amount for an intravenously administered or injected cancer chemotherapy agent covered under the health plan.</p>
        <p>(c) Nothing in this section shall be interpreted to prohibit a health plan company from requiring prior authorization or imposing other appropriate utilization controls in approving coverage for any chemotherapy.</p>
        <p>(d) A plan offered by the commissioner of management and budget under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/43A.23">43A.23 </a>is deemed to be at parity and in compliance with this section.</p>
        <p>(e) A health plan company is in compliance with this section if it does not include orally administered anticancer medication in the fourth tier of its pharmacy benefit.</p>
        <p>(f) A health plan company that provides coverage under a health plan for cancer chemotherapy treatment must indicate the level of coverage for orally administered anticancer medication within its pharmacy benefit filing with the commissioner.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.3075.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/326/#laws.0.1.0">2010 c 326 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/6/#laws.5.3.0">1Sp2017 c 6 art 5 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.308">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.308 HOSPITALIZATION AND ANESTHESIA FOR DENTAL PROCEDURES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.308.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope of coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to a health plan as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.011">62A.011</a> that provides coverage to a Minnesota resident.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.308.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Required coverages.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A health plan included in subdivision 1 must cover anesthesia and hospital charges for dental care provided to a covered person who: (1) is a child under age five; or (2) is severely disabled; or (3) has a medical condition and who requires hospitalization or general anesthesia for dental care treatment. A health carrier may require prior authorization of hospitalization for dental care procedures in the same manner that prior authorization is required for hospitalization for other covered diseases or conditions.</p>
          <p>(b) A health plan included in subdivision 1 must also provide coverage for general anesthesia and treatment rendered by a dentist for a medical condition covered by the health plan, regardless of whether the services are provided in a hospital or a dental office.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.308.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/91/#laws.0.1.0">1995 c 91 s 1</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.62A.309"><b>62A.309</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.7.8.0">2004 c 288 art 7 s 8</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.3091">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.3091 NONDISCRIMINATE COVERAGE OF TESTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3091.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope of requirement.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to any of the following if issued or renewed to a Minnesota resident or to cover a Minnesota resident:</p>
          <p>(1) a health plan, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.011">62A.011</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) coverage described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.011#stat.62A.011.3">62A.011, subdivision 3</a>, clauses (2), (3), or (6) to (12); and</p>
          <p>(3) a policy, contract, or certificate issued by a community integrated service network licensed under chapter 62N.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3091.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Requirement.</span></h2>
          <p>Coverage described in subdivision 1 that covers laboratory tests, diagnostic tests, and x-rays must provide the same coverage, without requiring additional signatures, for all such tests ordered by an advanced practice registered nurse operating pursuant to chapter 148 or a physician assistant practicing pursuant to chapter 147A. Nothing in this section shall be construed to interfere with any written agreement between a physician and an advanced practice registered nurse or between a physician and a physician assistant.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.3091.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/446/#laws.1.24.0">1996 c 446 art 1 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/225/#laws.2.62.0">1997 c 225 art 2 s 62</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/58/#laws.0.4.0">2022 c 58 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/25/#laws.0.6.0">2023 c 25 s 6</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.3092">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.3092 EQUAL TREATMENT OF SURGICAL FIRST ASSISTING SERVICES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3092.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope of requirement.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to any of the following if issued or renewed to a Minnesota resident or to cover a Minnesota resident:</p>
          <p>(1) a health plan, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.011">62A.011</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) coverage described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.011#stat.62A.011.3">62A.011, subdivision 3</a>, clauses (2), (3), or (6) to (12); and</p>
          <p>(3) a policy, contract, or certificate issued by a community integrated service network licensed under chapter 62N.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3092.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Requirement.</span></h2>
          <p>Coverage described in subdivision 1 that provides for payment for surgical first assisting benefits or services shall be construed as providing for payment for a registered nurse who performs first assistant functions and services that are within the scope of practice of a registered nurse.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.3092.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/446/#laws.1.25.0">1996 c 446 art 1 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/225/#laws.2.62.0">1997 c 225 art 2 s 62</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.3093">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.3093 COVERAGE FOR DIABETES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3093.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Required coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>A health plan, including a plan providing the coverage specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.011#stat.62A.011.3">62A.011, subdivision 3</a>, clause (10), must provide coverage for: (1) all physician prescribed medically appropriate and necessary equipment and supplies used in the management and treatment of diabetes; and (2) diabetes outpatient self-management training and education, including medical nutrition therapy, that is provided by a certified, registered, or licensed health care professional working in a program consistent with the national standards of diabetes self-management education as established by the American Diabetes Association. Coverage must include persons with gestational, type I or type II diabetes. Coverage required under this section is subject to the same deductible or coinsurance provisions applicable to the plan's hospital, medical expense, medical equipment, or prescription drug benefits. A health carrier may not reduce or eliminate coverage due to this requirement.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3093.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Medicare Part D exception.</span></h2>
          <p>A health plan providing the coverage specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.011">62A.011</a>, subdivision 3, clause (10), is not subject to the requirements of subdivision 1, clause (1), with respect to equipment and supplies covered under the Medicare Part D Prescription Drug program, whether or not the covered person is enrolled in a Medicare Part D plan.</p>
          <p>This subdivision does not apply to a health plan providing the coverage specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.011">62A.011</a>, subdivision 3, clause (10), that was in effect on December 31, 2005, if the covered person remains enrolled in the plan and does not enroll in a Medicare Part D plan.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.3093.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/538/#laws.0.1.0">1994 c 538 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/52/#laws.0.1.0">1995 c 52 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/57/#laws.0.1.0">1997 c 57 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/255/#laws.0.12.0">2006 c 255 s 12</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.3094">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.3094 COVERAGE FOR AUTISM SPECTRUM DISORDERS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3094.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of this section, the terms defined in paragraphs (b) to (d) have the meanings given.</p>
          <p>(b) "Autism spectrum disorders" means the conditions as determined by criteria set forth in the most recent edition of the Diagnostic and Statistical Manual of Mental Disorders of the American Psychiatric Association.</p>
          <p>(c) "Medically necessary care" means health care services appropriate, in terms of type, frequency, level, setting, and duration, to the enrollee's condition, and diagnostic testing and preventative services. Medically necessary care must be consistent with generally accepted practice parameters as determined by physicians and licensed psychologists who typically manage patients who have autism spectrum disorders.</p>
          <p>(d) "Mental health professional" means a mental health professional who is qualified according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245I.04#stat.245I.04.2">245I.04, subdivision 2</a>, clause (1), (2), (3), (4), or (6), who has training and expertise in autism spectrum disorder and child development.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3094.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Coverage required.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A health plan issued to a large employer, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62Q.18#stat.62Q.18.1">62Q.18, subdivision 1</a>, must provide coverage for the diagnosis, evaluation, multidisciplinary assessment, and medically necessary care of children under 18 with autism spectrum disorders, including but not limited to the following:</p>
          <p>(1) early intensive behavioral and developmental therapy based in behavioral and developmental science, including, but not limited to, all types of applied behavior analysis, intensive early intervention behavior therapy, and intensive behavior intervention;</p>
          <p>(2) neurodevelopmental and behavioral health treatments and management;</p>
          <p>(3) speech therapy;</p>
          <p>(4) occupational therapy;</p>
          <p>(5) physical therapy; and</p>
          <p>(6) medications.</p>
          <p>(b) The diagnosis, evaluation, and assessment must include an assessment of the child's developmental skills, functional behavior, needs, and capacities.</p>
          <p>(c) The coverage required under this subdivision must include treatment that is in accordance with an individualized treatment plan prescribed by the enrollee's treating physician or mental health professional.</p>
          <p>(d) A health carrier may not refuse to renew or reissue, or otherwise terminate or restrict, coverage of an individual solely because the individual is diagnosed with an autism spectrum disorder.</p>
          <p>(e) A health carrier may request an updated treatment plan only once every six months, unless the health carrier and the treating physician or mental health professional agree that a more frequent review is necessary due to emerging circumstances.</p>
          <p>(f) An independent progress evaluation conducted by a mental health professional with expertise and training in autism spectrum disorder and child development must be completed to determine if progress toward function and generalizable gains, as determined in the treatment plan, is being made.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3094.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">No effect on other law.</span></h2>
          <p>Nothing in this section limits the coverage required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62Q.47">62Q.47</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3094.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">State health care programs.</span></h2>
          <p>This section does not affect benefits available under the medical assistance and MinnesotaCare programs and does not limit, restrict, or otherwise reduce coverage under these programs.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.3094.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/108/#laws.12.3.0">2013 c 108 art 12 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/0/30/#laws.17.2.0">2021 c 30 art 17 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>The amendment to subdivision 1 by Laws 2021, chapter 30, article 17, section 2, is effective upon federal approval. The commissioner of human services shall notify the revisor of statutes when federal approval is obtained. Laws 2021, chapter 30, article 17, section 114.</p>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.3095">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.3095 PRESCRIPTION EYE DROPS COVERAGE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3095.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope of coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to all health plans, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.011#stat.62A.011.3">62A.011, subdivision 3</a>, that provide coverage to Minnesota residents.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3095.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Required coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Every health plan included in subdivision 1 that provides coverage for prescription eye drops shall not deny coverage for a refill of the prescription eye drops if the refill is requested by the person insured by the health plan, pursuant to paragraph (b), and if the prescribing practitioner indicated on the original prescription that additional quantities are needed and the refill requested by the insured does not exceed the number of additional quantities needed.</p>
          <p>(b) Coverage shall only be provided pursuant to paragraph (a) if the insured makes a refill request for:</p>
          <p>(1) a 30-day refill supply and the request is made between 21 and 30 days from the later of:</p>
          <p>(i) the original date that the prescription was distributed to the insured; or</p>
          <p>(ii) the date the most recent refill was distributed to the insured; or</p>
          <p>(2) a 90-day refill supply and the request is made between 75 and 90 days from the later of:</p>
          <p>(i) the original date that the prescription was distributed to the insured; or</p>
          <p>(ii) the date the most recent refill was distributed to the insured.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.3095.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/47/#laws.0.1.0">2017 c 47 s 1</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.3097">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.3097 PEDIATRIC AUTOIMMUNE NEUROPSYCHIATRIC DISORDERS ASSOCIATED WITH STREPTOCOCCAL INFECTIONS (PANDAS) AND PEDIATRIC ACUTE-ONSET NEUROPSYCHIATRIC SYNDROME (PANS) TREATMENT; COVERAGE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3097.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The definitions in this subdivision apply to this section.</p>
          <p>(b) "Pediatric acute-onset neuropsychiatric syndrome" means a class of acute-onset obsessive compulsive or tic disorders or other behavioral changes presenting in children and adolescents that are not otherwise explained by another known neurologic or medical disorder.</p>
          <p>(c) "Pediatric autoimmune neuropsychiatric disorders associated with streptococcal infections" means a condition in which a streptococcal infection in a child or adolescent causes the abrupt onset of clinically significant obsessions, compulsions, tics, or other neuropsychiatric symptoms or behavioral changes, or a relapsing and remitting course of symptom severity.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3097.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Scope of coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to all health plans that provide coverage to Minnesota residents.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3097.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Required coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>Every health plan included in subdivision 2 must provide coverage for treatment for pediatric autoimmune neuropsychiatric disorders associated with streptococcal infections (PANDAS) and for treatment for pediatric acute-onset neuropsychiatric syndrome (PANS). Treatments that must be covered under this section must be recommended by the insured's licensed health care professional and include but are not limited to antibiotics, medication and behavioral therapies to manage neuropsychiatric symptoms, plasma exchange, and immunoglobulin.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3097.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Reimbursement.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of commerce shall reimburse health carriers for coverage under this section. Reimbursement is available only for coverage that would not have been provided by the health carrier without the requirements of this section. Each fiscal year an amount necessary to make payments to health carriers to defray the cost of providing coverage under this section is appropriated to the commissioner of commerce. Health carriers shall report to the commissioner quantified costs attributable to the additional benefit under this section in a format developed by the commissioner. The commissioner shall evaluate submissions and make payments to health carriers as provided in Code of Federal Regulations, title 45, section 155.170.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.3097.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/9/#laws.8.2.0">1Sp2019 c 9 art 8 s 2</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.3098">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.3098 RAPID WHOLE GENOME SEQUENCING; COVERAGE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3098.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definition.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of this section, "rapid whole genome sequencing" or "rWGS" means an investigation of the entire human genome, including coding and noncoding regions and mitochondrial deoxyribonucleic acid, to identify disease-causing genetic changes that returns the final results in 14 days. Rapid whole genome sequencing includes patient-only whole genome sequencing and duo and trio whole genome sequencing of the patient and the patient's biological parent or parents.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3098.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Required coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>A health plan that provides coverage to Minnesota residents must cover rWGS testing if the enrollee:</p>
          <p>(1) is 21 years of age or younger;</p>
          <p>(2) has a complex or acute illness of unknown etiology that is not confirmed to have been caused by an environmental exposure, toxic ingestion, an infection with a normal response to therapy, or trauma; and</p>
          <p>(3) is receiving inpatient hospital services in an intensive care unit or a neonatal or high acuity pediatric care unit.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3098.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Coverage criteria.</span></h2>
          <p>Coverage may be based on the following medical necessity criteria:</p>
          <p>(1) the enrollee has symptoms that suggest a broad differential diagnosis that would require an evaluation by multiple genetic tests if rWGS testing is not performed;</p>
          <p>(2) timely identification of a molecular diagnosis is necessary in order to guide clinical decision making, and the rWGS testing may aid in guiding the treatment or management of the enrollee's condition; and</p>
          <p>(3) the enrollee's complex or acute illness of unknown etiology includes at least one of the following conditions:</p>
          <p>(i) congenital anomalies involving at least two organ systems, or complex or multiple congenital anomalies in one organ system;</p>
          <p>(ii) specific organ malformations that are highly suggestive of a genetic etiology;</p>
          <p>(iii) abnormal laboratory tests or abnormal chemistry profiles suggesting the presence of a genetic disease, complex metabolic disorder, or inborn error of metabolism;</p>
          <p>(iv) refractory or severe hypoglycemia or hyperglycemia;</p>
          <p>(v) abnormal response to therapy related to an underlying medical condition affecting vital organs or bodily systems;</p>
          <p>(vi) severe muscle weakness, rigidity, or spasticity;</p>
          <p>(vii) refractory seizures;</p>
          <p>(viii) a high-risk stratification on evaluation for a brief resolved unexplained event with any of the following features:</p>
          <p>(A) a recurrent event without respiratory infection;</p>
          <p>(B) a recurrent seizure-like event; or</p>
          <p>(C) a recurrent cardiopulmonary resuscitation;</p>
          <p>(ix) abnormal cardiac diagnostic testing results that are suggestive of possible channelopathies, arrhythmias, cardiomyopathies, myocarditis, or structural heart disease;</p>
          <p>(x) abnormal diagnostic imaging studies that are suggestive of underlying genetic condition;</p>
          <p>(xi) abnormal physiologic function studies that are suggestive of an underlying genetic etiology; or</p>
          <p>(xii) family genetic history related to the patient's condition.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3098.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Cost sharing.</span></h2>
          <p>Coverage provided in this section is subject to the enrollee's health plan cost-sharing requirements, including any deductibles, co-payments, or coinsurance requirements that apply to diagnostic testing services.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3098.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Payment for services provided.</span></h2>
          <p>If the enrollee's health plan uses a capitated or bundled payment arrangement to reimburse a provider for services provided in an inpatient setting, reimbursement for services covered under this section must be paid separately and in addition to any reimbursement otherwise payable to the provider under the capitated or bundled payment arrangement, unless the health carrier and the provider have negotiated an increased capitated or bundled payment rate that includes the services covered under this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3098.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Genetic data.</span></h2>
          <p>Genetic data generated as a result of performing rWGS and covered under this section: (1) must be used for the primary purpose of assisting the ordering provider and treating care team to diagnose and treat the patient; (2) is protected health information as set forth under the Health Insurance Portability and Accountability Act (HIPAA), the Health Information Technology for Economic and Clinical Health Act, and any promulgated regulations, including but not limited to Code of Federal Regulations, title 45, parts 160 and 164, subparts A and E; and (3) is a protected health record under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.291">144.291</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.298">144.298</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3098.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Reimbursement.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner of commerce must reimburse health carriers for coverage under this section. Reimbursement is available only for coverage that would not have been provided by the health plan without the requirements of this section. Treatments and services covered by the health plan as of January 1, 2024, are ineligible for payments under this subdivision by the commissioner of commerce.</p>
          <p>(b) Health carriers must report to the commissioner of commerce quantified costs attributable to the additional benefit under this section in a format developed by the commissioner. A health plan's coverage as of January 1, 2024, must be used by the health carrier as the basis for determining whether coverage would not have been provided by the health plan for purposes of this subdivision.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner of commerce must evaluate submissions and make payments to health carriers as provided in Code of Federal Regulations, title 45, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/155.170">155.170</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3098.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Appropriation.</span></h2>
          <p>Each fiscal year, an amount necessary to make payments to health carriers to defray the cost of providing coverage under this section is appropriated to the commissioner of commerce.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.3098.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.57.6.0">2024 c 127 art 57 s 6</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">MEDICARE SUPPLEMENT INSURANCE</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.3099">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.3099 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3099.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope.</span></h2>
          <p>The definitions provided in this section apply to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.3099">62A.3099</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.44">62A.44</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3099.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Accident, accidental injury, accidental means.</span></h2>
          <p>"Accident," "accidental injury," or "accidental means" means to employ "result" language and does not include words that establish an accidental means test or use words such as "external," "violent," "visible wounds," or similar words of description or characterization.</p>
          <p>(1) The definition shall not be more restrictive than the following: "Injury or injuries for which benefits are provided means accidental bodily injury sustained by the insured person which is the direct result of an accident, independent of disease or bodily infirmity or any other cause, and occurs while insurance coverage is in force."</p>
          <p>(2) The definition may provide that injuries shall not include injuries for which benefits are provided or available under a workers' compensation, employer's liability or similar law, or motor vehicle no-fault plan, unless prohibited by law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3099.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Applicant. </span></h2>
          <p>"Applicant" means:</p>
          <p>(1) in the case of an individual Medicare supplement policy or certificate, the person who seeks to contract for insurance benefits; and</p>
          <p>(2) in the case of a group Medicare supplement policy or certificate, the proposed certificate holder.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3099.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Bankruptcy. </span></h2>
          <p>"Bankruptcy" means a situation in which a Medicare Advantage organization that is not an issuer has filed, or has had filed against it, a petition for declaration of bankruptcy and has ceased doing business in the state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3099.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Benefit period, Medicare benefit period. </span></h2>
          <p>"Benefit period" or "Medicare benefit period" shall not be defined more restrictively than as defined in the Medicare program.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3099.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Certificate. </span></h2>
          <p>"Certificate" means a certificate delivered or issued for delivery in this state or offered to a resident of this state under a group Medicare supplement policy or certificate.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3099.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Certificate form. </span></h2>
          <p>"Certificate form" means the form on which the certificate is delivered or issued for delivery by the issuer.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3099.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Convalescent nursing home, extended care facility, skilled nursing facility. </span></h2>
          <p>"Convalescent nursing home," "extended care facility," or "skilled nursing facility" shall not be defined more restrictively than as defined in the Medicare program.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3099.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Employee welfare benefit plan. </span></h2>
          <p> "Employee welfare benefit plan" means a plan, fund, or program of employee benefits as defined in United States Code, title 29, section 1002 (Employee Retirement Income Security Act).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3099.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Health care expenses. </span></h2>
          <p>"Health care expenses" means, for purposes of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.36">62A.36</a>, expenses of health maintenance organizations associated with the delivery of health care services which are analogous to incurred losses of insurers. The expenses shall not include:</p>
          <p>(1) home office and overhead costs;</p>
          <p>(2) advertising costs;</p>
          <p>(3) commissions and other acquisition costs;</p>
          <p>(4) taxes;</p>
          <p>(5) capital costs;</p>
          <p>(6) administrative costs; and</p>
          <p>(7) claims processing costs.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3099.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Hospital.</span></h2>
          <p>"Hospital" may be defined in relation to its status, facilities, and available services or to reflect its accreditation by the Joint Commission on Accreditation of Hospitals, but not more restrictively than as defined in the Medicare program.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3099.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Insolvency.</span></h2>
          <p>"Insolvency" means a situation in which an issuer, licensed to transact the business of insurance in this state, including the right to transact business as any type of issuer, has had a final order of liquidation entered against it with a finding of insolvency by a court of competent jurisdiction in the issuer's state of domicile.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3099.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Issuer.</span></h2>
          <p>"Issuer" includes insurance companies, fraternal benefit societies, health service plan corporations, health maintenance organizations, and any other entity delivering or issuing for delivery Medicare supplement policies or certificates in this state or offering these policies or certificates to residents of this state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3099.14">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Medicare.</span></h2>
          <p>"Medicare" shall be defined in the policy and certificate. Medicare may be defined as the Health Insurance for the Aged Act, title XVIII of the Social Security Amendments of 1965, as amended, or title I, part I, of Public Law 89-97, as enacted by the 89th Congress of the United States of America and popularly known as the Health Insurance for the Aged Act, as amended.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3099.15">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Medicare Advantage plan.</span></h2>
          <p>"Medicare Advantage plan" means a plan of coverage for health benefits under Medicare Part C as defined in section 1859 of the federal Social Security Act, United States Code, title 42, section 1395w-28, and includes:</p>
          <p>(1) coordinated care plans which provide health care services, including, but not limited to, health maintenance organization plans, with or without a point-of-service option, plans offered by provider-sponsored organizations, and preferred provider organization plans;</p>
          <p>(2) medical savings account plans coupled with a contribution into a Medicare Advantage medical savings account; and</p>
          <p>(3) Medicare Advantage private fee-for-service plans.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3099.16">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Medicare eligible expenses.</span></h2>
          <p>"Medicare eligible expenses" means health care expenses covered by Medicare Part A or B, to the extent recognized as reasonable and medically necessary by Medicare.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3099.17">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.<span class="headnote">Medicare-related coverage.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Medicare-related coverage" means a policy, contract, or certificate issued as a supplement to Medicare, regulated under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.3099">62A.3099</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.44">62A.44</a>, including Medicare select coverage; policies, contracts, or certificates that supplement Medicare issued by health maintenance organizations; or policies, contracts, or certificates governed by section 1833 (known as "HCPP" contracts) or 1876 (known as "Cost" contracts) of the federal Social Security Act, United States Code, title 42, section 1395, et seq., as amended; or Section 4001 of the Balanced Budget Act of 1997 (BBA)(Public Law 105-33), Sections 1851 to 1859 of the Social Security Act establishing Part C of the Medicare program, known as the "Medicare Advantage program."</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3099.18">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.<span class="headnote">Medicare supplement policy or certificate.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Medicare supplement policy or certificate" means a group or individual policy of accident and sickness insurance or a subscriber contract of hospital and medical service associations or health maintenance organizations, other than those policies or certificates covered by section 1876 of the federal Social Security Act, United States Code, title 42, section 1395, et seq., or an issued policy under a demonstration project specified under amendments to the federal Social Security Act, which is advertised, marketed, or designed primarily as a supplement to reimbursements under Medicare for the hospital, medical, or surgical expenses of persons eligible for Medicare or as a supplement to Medicare Advantage plans established under Medicare Part C. "Medicare supplement policy" does not include Medicare Advantage plans established under Medicare Part C, outpatient prescription drug plans established under Medicare Part D, any health care prepayment plan that provides benefits under an agreement under section 1833(a)(1)(A) of the Social Security Act, or any policy issued to an employer or employers or to the trustee of a fund established by an employer where only employees or retirees, and dependents of employees or retirees, are eligible for coverage, or any policy issued to a labor union or similar employee organization.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.3099.18a">
