        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/263/#laws.0.14.0">1983 c 263 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/554/#laws.1.13.0">1992 c 554 art 1 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/13/#laws.1.18.0">1993 c 13 art 1 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/330/#laws.0.10.0">1993 c 330 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/446/#laws.1.35.0">1996 c 446 art 1 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/344/#laws.0.12.0">2008 c 344 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/57/#laws.2.15.0">2023 c 57 art 2 s 15</a></p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>The amendment to subdivision 2 by Laws 2023, chapter 57, article 2, section 15, is effective August 1, 2026. Laws 2023, chapter 57, article 2, section 15, the effective date, as amended by Laws 2024, chapter 114, article 6, section 9.</p>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.62A.45"><b>62A.45</b> [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.3093">62A.3093</a>]
</div>
      <h1 class="fgh">PRESCRIPTION DRUG PLANS</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.451">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.451 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.451.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Applicability.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.451">62A.451</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4528">62A.4528</a>, the terms defined in this section have the meanings given.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.451.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p>"Commissioner" means the commissioner of commerce.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.451.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Enrollee.</span></h2>
          <p>"Enrollee" means an individual who is entitled to limited health services under a contract with an entity authorized to provide or arrange for such services under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.451">62A.451</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4528">62A.4528</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.451.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Evidence of coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>"Evidence of coverage" means the certificate, agreement, or contract issued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4516">62A.4516</a> setting forth the coverage to which an enrollee is entitled.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.451.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Limited health service.</span></h2>
          <p>"Limited health service" means pharmaceutical services covered under Medicare Part D. Limited health service does not include hospital, medical, surgical, or emergency services.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.451.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Prepaid limited health service organization.</span></h2>
          <p>"Prepaid limited health service organization" means any corporation, partnership, or other entity that, in return for a prepayment, undertakes to provide or arrange for the provision of limited health services to enrollees. Prepaid limited health service organization does not include:</p>
          <p>(1) an entity otherwise authorized under the laws of this state either to provide any limited health service on a prepayment or other basis or to indemnify for any limited health service;</p>
          <p>(2) an entity that meets the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4514">62A.4514</a>; or</p>
          <p>(3) a provider or entity when providing or arranging for the provision of limited health services under a contract with a prepaid limited health service organization or with an entity described in clause (1) or (2).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.451.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Provider.</span></h2>
          <p>"Provider" means a physician, pharmacist, health facility, or other person or institution that is licensed or otherwise authorized to deliver or furnish limited health services under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.451">62A.451</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4528">62A.4528</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.451.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Subscriber.</span></h2>
          <p>"Subscriber" means the person whose employment or other status, except for family dependency, is the basis for entitlement to limited health services under a contract with an entity authorized to provide or arrange for such services under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.451">62A.451</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4528">62A.4528</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.451.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/17/#laws.2.1.0">2005 c 17 art 2 s 1</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.4511">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.4511 CERTIFICATE OF AUTHORITY REQUIRED.</h1>
        <p>No person, corporation, partnership, or other entity may operate a prepaid limited health service organization in this state without obtaining and maintaining a certificate of authority from the commissioner under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.451">62A.451</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4528">62A.4528</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.4511.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/17/#laws.2.2.0">2005 c 17 art 2 s 2</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.4512">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.4512 APPLICATION FOR CERTIFICATE OF AUTHORITY.</h1>
        <p>An application for a certificate of authority to operate a prepaid limited health service organization must be filed with the commissioner on a form prescribed by the commissioner. The application must be verified by an officer or authorized representative of the applicant and must set forth, or be accompanied by, the following:</p>
        <p>(1) a copy of the applicant's basic organizational document, such as the articles of incorporation, articles of association, partnership agreement, trust agreement, or other applicable documents and all amendments to these documents;</p>
        <p>(2) a copy of all bylaws, rules and regulations, or similar documents, if any, regulating the conduct of the applicant's internal affairs;</p>
        <p>(3) a list of the names, addresses, official positions, and biographical information of the individuals who are responsible for conducting the applicant's affairs, including but not limited to, all members of the board of directors, board of trustees, executive committee, or other governing board or committee, the principal officers, and any person or entity owning or having the right to acquire ten percent or more of the voting securities of the applicant, and the partners or members in the case of a partnership or association;</p>
        <p>(4) a statement generally describing the applicant, its facilities, personnel, and the limited health services to be offered;</p>
        <p>(5) a copy of the form of any contract made or to be made between the applicant and any providers regarding the provision of limited health services to enrollees;</p>
        <p>(6) a copy of the form of any contract made or to be made between the applicant and any person listed in clause (3);</p>
        <p>(7) a copy of the form of any contract made or to be made between the applicant and any person, corporation, partnership, or other entity for the performance on the applicant's behalf of any functions including, but not limited to, marketing, administration, enrollment, investment management, and subcontracting for the provision of limited health services to enrollees;</p>
        <p>(8) a copy of the form of any group contract that is to be issued to employers, unions, trustees, or other organizations and a copy of any form of evidence of coverage to be issued to subscribers;</p>
        <p>(9) a copy of the applicant's most recent financial statements audited by independent certified public accountants. If the financial affairs of the applicant's parent company are audited by independent certified public accountants but those of the applicant are not, then a copy of the most recent audited financial statement of the applicant's parent company, certified by an independent certified public accountant, attached to which shall be consolidating financial statements of the applicant, satisfies this requirement unless the commissioner determines that additional or more recent financial information is required for the proper administration of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.451">62A.451</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4528">62A.4528</a>;</p>
        <p>(10) a copy of the applicant's financial plan, including a three-year projection of anticipated operating results, a statement of the sources of working capital, and any other sources of funding and provisions for contingencies;</p>
        <p>(11) a statement acknowledging that all lawful process in any legal action or proceeding against the applicant on a cause of action arising in this state is valid if served in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/45.028">45.028</a>;</p>
        <p>(12) a description of how the applicant will comply with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4523">62A.4523</a>; and</p>
        <p>(13) such other information as the commissioner may reasonably require to make the determinations required by sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.451">62A.451</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4528">62A.4528</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.4512.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/17/#laws.2.3.0">2005 c 17 art 2 s 3</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.4513">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.4513 ISSUANCE OF CERTIFICATE OF AUTHORITY; DENIAL.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.4513.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Issuance.</span></h2>
          <p>Following receipt of an application filed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4512">62A.4512</a>, the commissioner shall review the application and notify the applicant of any deficiencies. The commissioner must approve or deny an application within 90 days after receipt of a substantially complete application, or the application is deemed approved. The commissioner shall issue a certificate of authority to an applicant provided that the following conditions are met:</p>
          <p>(1) the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4512">62A.4512</a> have been fulfilled;</p>
          <p>(2) the individuals responsible for conducting the applicant's affairs are competent, trustworthy, and possess good reputations, and have had appropriate experience, training, or education;</p>
          <p>(3) the applicant is financially responsible and may reasonably be expected to meet its obligations to enrollees and to prospective enrollees. In making this determination, the commissioner may consider:</p>
          <p>(i) the financial soundness of the applicant's arrangements for limited health services;</p>
          <p>(ii) the adequacy of working capital, other sources of funding, and provisions for contingencies;</p>
          <p>(iii) any agreement for paying the cost of the limited health services or for alternative coverage in the event of insolvency of the prepaid limited health service organization; and</p>
          <p>(iv) the manner in which the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4523">62A.4523</a> have been fulfilled; and</p>
          <p>(4) any deficiencies identified by the commissioner have been corrected.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.4513.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Denials.</span></h2>
          <p>If the certificate of authority is denied, the commissioner shall notify the applicant and shall specify the reasons for denial in the notice. The prepaid limited health service organization has 30 days from the date of receipt of the notice to request a hearing before the commissioner under chapter 14.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.4513.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/17/#laws.2.4.0">2005 c 17 art 2 s 4</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.4514">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.4514 FILING REQUIREMENTS FOR AUTHORIZED ENTITIES.</h1>
        <p>(a) An entity authorized under the laws of this state to operate a health maintenance organization, an accident and health insurance company, a nonprofit health service plan corporation, a fraternal benefit society, or a multiple employer welfare arrangement, and that is not otherwise authorized under the laws of this state to offer limited health services on a per capita or fixed prepayment basis, may do so by filing for approval with the commissioner the information requested by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4512">62A.4512</a>, clauses (4), (5), (7), (8), and (10), and any subsequent material modification or addition to those provisions.</p>
        <p>(b) If the commissioner disapproves the filing, the procedures provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4513#stat.62A.4513.2">62A.4513, subdivision 2</a>, must be followed.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.4514.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/17/#laws.2.5.0">2005 c 17 art 2 s 5</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.4515">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.4515 MATERIAL MODIFICATIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.4515.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Material modifications.</span></h2>
          <p>A prepaid limited health service organization shall file with the commissioner prior to use, a notice of any material modification of any matter or document furnished under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4512">62A.4512</a>, together with supporting documents necessary to fully explain the modification. If the commissioner does not disapprove the filing within 60 days of its filing, the filing is deemed approved.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.4515.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Procedure for disapproval.</span></h2>
          <p>If a filing under this section is disapproved, the commissioner shall notify the prepaid limited health service organization and specify the reasons for disapproval in the notice. The prepaid limited health service organization has 30 days from the date of receipt of notice to request a hearing before the commissioner under chapter 14.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.4515.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/17/#laws.2.6.0">2005 c 17 art 2 s 6</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.4516">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.4516 EVIDENCE OF COVERAGE.</h1>
        <p>Every subscriber must be issued an evidence of coverage consistent with the requirements of Medicare Part D.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.4516.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/17/#laws.2.7.0">2005 c 17 art 2 s 7</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.4517">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.4517 CONSTRUCTION WITH OTHER LAWS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.4517.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Application of other insurance laws.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A prepaid limited health service organization organized under the laws of this state is deemed to be a domestic insurer for purposes of chapter 60D unless specifically exempted in writing from one or more of the provisions of that chapter by the commissioner, based upon a determination that the provision is not applicable to the organization or to providing coverage under Medicare Part D.</p>
          <p>(b) No other provision of chapters 60 to 72C applies to a prepaid limited health service organization unless such an organization is specifically mentioned in the provision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.4517.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Not a healing art.</span></h2>
          <p>The provision of limited health services by a prepaid limited health service organization or other entity under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.451">62A.451</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4528">62A.4528</a> must not be deemed to be the practice of medicine or other healing arts.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.4517.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Solicitation and advertising.</span></h2>
          <p>Solicitation to arrange for or provide limited health services in accordance with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.451">62A.451</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4528">62A.4528</a> shall not be construed to violate any provision of law relating to solicitation or advertising by health professionals.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.4517.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/17/#laws.2.8.0">2005 c 17 art 2 s 8</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.4518">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.4518 NONDUPLICATION OF COVERAGE.</h1>
        <p>Notwithstanding any other law of this state, a prepaid limited health service organization, health maintenance organization, accident and health insurance company, nonprofit health service plan corporation, or fraternal benefit society may exclude, in any contract or policy issued to a group, any coverage that would duplicate the coverage for limited health services, whether in the form of services, supplies, or reimbursement, insofar as the coverage or service is provided in accordance with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.451">62A.451</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4528">62A.4528</a> under a contract or policy issued to the same group or to a part of that group by a prepaid limited health service organization, a health maintenance organization, an accident and health insurance company, a nonprofit health service corporation, or a fraternal benefit society.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.4518.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/17/#laws.2.9.0">2005 c 17 art 2 s 9</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.4519">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.4519 COMPLAINT SYSTEM.</h1>
        <p>Every prepaid limited health service organization shall establish and maintain a complaint system providing reasonable procedures for resolving written complaints initiated by enrollees and providers, consistent with the requirements of Medicare Part D.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.4519.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/17/#laws.2.10.0">2005 c 17 art 2 s 10</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.4520">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.4520 EXAMINATION OF ORGANIZATION.</h1>
        <p>(a) The commissioner may examine the affairs of any prepaid limited health service organization as often as is reasonably necessary to protect the interests of the people of this state, but not less frequently than once every three years.</p>
        <p>(b) Every prepaid limited health service organization shall make its relevant books and records available for an examination and in every way cooperate with the commissioner to facilitate an examination.</p>
        <p>(c) In lieu of an examination, the commissioner may accept the report of an examination made by the commissioner of another state.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.4520.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/17/#laws.2.11.0">2005 c 17 art 2 s 11</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.4521">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.4521 INVESTMENTS.</h1>
        <p>The funds of a prepaid limited health service organization shall be invested only in accordance with the guidelines under chapter 62D for investments by health maintenance organizations.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.4521.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/17/#laws.2.12.0">2005 c 17 art 2 s 12</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.4522">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.4522 AGENTS.</h1>
        <p>No individual may apply, procure, negotiate, or place for others any policy or contract of a prepaid limited health service organization unless that individual holds a license or is otherwise authorized to sell accident and health insurance policies, nonprofit health service plan contracts, or health maintenance organization contracts.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.4522.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/17/#laws.2.13.0">2005 c 17 art 2 s 13</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.4523">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.4523 PROTECTION AGAINST INSOLVENCY; DEPOSIT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.4523.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Net equity.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Except as approved in accordance with subdivision 4, each prepaid limited health service organization shall at all times have and maintain tangible net equity equal to the greater of:</p>
          <p>(1) $100,000; or</p>
          <p>(2) two percent of the organization's annual gross premium income, up to a maximum of the required capital and surplus of an accident and health insurer.</p>
          <p>(b) A prepaid limited health service organization that has uncovered expenses in excess of $100,000, as reported on the most recent annual financial statement filed with the commissioner, shall maintain tangible net equity equal to 25 percent of the uncovered expense in excess of $100,000 in addition to the tangible net equity required by paragraph (a).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.4523.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purpose of this section:</p>
          <p>(1) "net equity" means the excess of total assets over total liabilities, excluding liabilities which have been subordinated in a manner acceptable to the commissioner; and</p>
          <p>(2) "tangible net equity" means net equity reduced by the value assigned to intangible assets including, but not limited to, goodwill; going concern value; organizational expense; start-up costs; long-term prepayments of deferred charges; nonreturnable deposits; and obligations of officers, directors, owners, or affiliates, except short-term obligations of affiliates for goods or services arising in the normal course of business that are payable on the same terms as equivalent transactions with nonaffiliates and that are not past due.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.4523.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Deposit.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Each prepaid limited health service organization shall deposit with the commissioner or with any organization or trustee acceptable to the commissioner through which a custodial or controlled account is utilized, cash, securities, or any combination of these or other measures that is acceptable to the commissioner, in an amount equal to $50,000 plus 25 percent of the tangible net equity required in subdivision 1; provided, however, that the deposit must not be required to exceed $200,000.</p>
          <p>(b) The deposit is an admitted asset of the prepaid limited health service organization in the determination of tangible net equity.</p>
          <p>(c) All income from deposits is an asset of the prepaid limited health service organization. A prepaid limited health service organization may withdraw a deposit or any part of it after making a substitute deposit of equal amount and value. Any securities must be approved by the commissioner before being substituted.</p>
          <p>(d) The deposit must be used to protect the interests of the prepaid limited health service organization's enrollees and to ensure continuation of limited health care services to enrollees of a prepaid limited health service organization that is in rehabilitation or conservation. If a prepaid limited health service organization is placed in receivership or liquidation, the deposit is an asset subject to provisions of chapter 60B.</p>
          <p>(e) The commissioner may reduce or eliminate the deposit requirement if the prepaid limited health service organization has made an acceptable deposit with the state or jurisdiction of domicile for the protection of all enrollees, wherever located, and delivers to the commissioner a certificate to that effect, duly authenticated by the appropriate state official holding the deposit.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.4523.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Waiver of net equity requirement.</span></h2>
          <p>Upon application by a prepaid limited health service organization, the commissioner may waive some or all of the requirements of subdivision 1 for any period of time the commissioner deems proper upon a finding that either:</p>
          <p>(1) the prepaid limited health service organization has a net equity of at least $10,000,000; or</p>
          <p>(2) an entity having a net equity of at least $10,000,000 furnishes to the commissioner a written commitment, acceptable to the commissioner, to provide for the uncovered expenses of the prepaid limited health service organization.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.4523.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Definition; uncovered expenses.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purposes of this section, "uncovered expense" means the cost of health care services that are the obligation of a prepaid limited health organization (1) for which an enrollee may be liable in the event of the insolvency of the organization and (2) for which alternative arrangements acceptable to the commissioner have not been made to cover the costs. Costs incurred by a provider who has agreed in writing not to bill enrollees, except for permissible supplemental charges, must be considered a covered expense.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.4523.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/17/#laws.2.14.0">2005 c 17 art 2 s 14</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.4524">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.4524 OFFICER'S AND EMPLOYEE'S FIDELITY BOND.</h1>
        <p>(a) A prepaid limited health service organization shall maintain in force a fidelity bond in its own name on its officers and employees in an amount not less than $20,000,000 or in any other amount prescribed by the commissioner. Except as otherwise provided by this paragraph, the bond must be issued by an insurance company that is licensed to do business in this state or, if the fidelity bond required by this paragraph is not available from an insurance company that holds a certificate of authority in this state, a fidelity bond procured by a licensed surplus lines agent resident in this state in compliance with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/60A.195">60A.195</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/60A.2095">60A.2095</a> satisfies the requirements of this paragraph.</p>
        <p>(b) In lieu of the bond specified in paragraph (a), a prepaid limited health service organization may deposit with the commissioner cash or securities or other investments of the types set forth in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4521">62A.4521</a>. Such a deposit must be maintained by the commissioner in the amount and subject to the same conditions required for a bond under this paragraph.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.4524.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/17/#laws.2.15.0">2005 c 17 art 2 s 15</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.4525">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.4525 REPORTS.</h1>
        <p>(a) Every prepaid limited health service organization shall file with the commissioner annually, on or before April 1, a report verified by at least two principal officers covering the preceding calendar year.</p>
        <p>(b) The report must be on forms prescribed by the commissioner and must include:</p>
        <p>(1) a financial statement of the organization, including its balance sheet, income statement, and statement of changes in financial position for the preceding year, certified by an independent public accountant, or a consolidated audited financial statement of its parent company certified by an independent public accountant, attached to which must be consolidating financial statements of the prepaid limited health service organization;</p>
        <p>(2) the number of subscribers at the beginning of the year, the number of subscribers at the end of the year, and the number of enrollments terminated during the year; and</p>
        <p>(3) such other information relating to the performance of the organization as is necessary to enable the commissioner to carry out the commissioner's duties under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.451">62A.451</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4528">62A.4528</a>.</p>
        <p>(c) The commissioner may require more frequent reports containing information necessary to enable the commissioner to carry out the commissioner's duties under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.451">62A.451</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4528">62A.4528</a>.</p>
        <p>(d) The commissioner may suspend the organization's certificate of authority pending the proper filing of the required report by the organization.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.4525.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/17/#laws.2.16.0">2005 c 17 art 2 s 16</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.4526">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.4526 SUSPENSION OR REVOCATION OF CERTIFICATE OF AUTHORITY.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.4526.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Grounds for suspension or revocation.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may suspend or revoke the certificate of authority issued to a prepaid limited health service organization under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.451">62A.451</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4528">62A.4528</a> upon determining that any of the following conditions exist:</p>
          <p>(1) the prepaid limited health service organization is operating significantly in contravention of its basic organizational document or in a manner contrary to that described in and reasonably inferred from any other information submitted under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4512">62A.4512</a>, unless amendments to the submissions have been filed with and approved by the commissioner;</p>
          <p>(2) the prepaid limited health service organization issues an evidence of coverage that does not comply with the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4516">62A.4516</a>;</p>
          <p>(3) the prepaid limited health service organization is unable to fulfill its obligations to furnish limited health services;</p>
          <p>(4) the prepaid limited health service organization is not financially responsible and may reasonably be expected to be unable to meet its obligations to enrollees or prospective enrollees;</p>
          <p>(5) the tangible net equity of the prepaid limited health service organization is less than that required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4523">62A.4523</a> or the prepaid limited health service organization has failed to correct any deficiency in its tangible net equity as required by the commissioner;</p>
          <p>(6) the prepaid limited health service organization has failed to implement in a reasonable manner the complaint system required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4519">62A.4519</a>;</p>
          <p>(7) the continued operation of the prepaid limited health service organization would be hazardous to its enrollees; or</p>
          <p>(8) the prepaid limited health service organization has otherwise failed to comply with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.451">62A.451</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4528">62A.4528</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.4526.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Procedure for suspension or revocation.</span></h2>
          <p>If the commissioner has cause to believe that grounds for the suspension or revocation of a certificate of authority exist, the commissioner shall notify the prepaid limited health service organization in writing specifically stating the grounds for suspension or revocation and fixing a time not more than 60 days after the date of notification for a hearing on the matter in accordance with chapter 14.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.4526.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Winding up after revocation.</span></h2>
          <p>When the certificate of authority of a prepaid limited health service organization is revoked, the organization shall proceed, immediately following the effective date of the order of revocation, to wind up its affairs, and shall conduct no further business except as may be essential to the orderly conclusion of the affairs of the organization. It shall engage in no further advertising or solicitation whatsoever. The commissioner may, by written order, permit such further operation of the organization as the commissioner may find to be in the best interest of enrollees, to the end that enrollees will be afforded the greatest practical opportunity to obtain continuing limited health services.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.4526.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/17/#laws.2.17.0">2005 c 17 art 2 s 17</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.4527">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.4527 PENALTIES.</h1>
        <p>In lieu of any penalty specified elsewhere in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.451">62A.451</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4528">62A.4528</a>, or when no penalty is specifically provided, whenever a prepaid limited health service organization or other person, corporation, partnership, or entity subject to those sections has been found, pursuant to chapter 14, to have violated any provision of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.451">62A.451</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.4528">62A.4528</a>, the commissioner may:</p>
        <p>(1) issue and cause to be served upon the organization, person, or entity charged with the violation a copy of the findings and an order requiring the organization, person, or entity to cease and desist from engaging in the act or practice that constitutes the violation; and</p>
        <p>(2) impose a monetary penalty of not more than $1,000 for each violation, but not to exceed an aggregate penalty of $10,000.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.4527.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/17/#laws.2.18.0">2005 c 17 art 2 s 18</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.4528">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.4528 REHABILITATION, CONSERVATION, OR LIQUIDATION.</h1>
        <p>(a) Any rehabilitation, conservation, or liquidation of a prepaid limited health service organization must be deemed to be the rehabilitation, conservation, or liquidation of an insurance company and must be conducted under chapter 60B.</p>
        <p>(b) A prepaid limited health service organization is not subject to the laws and rules governing insurance insolvency guaranty funds, nor shall any insurance insolvency guaranty fund provide protection to individuals entitled to receive limited health services from a prepaid limited health service organization.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.4528.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/17/#laws.2.19.0">2005 c 17 art 2 s 19</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">LONG-TERM CARE POLICIES</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.46">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.46 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.46.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Applicability.</span></h2>
          <p>The definitions in this section apply to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.46">62A.46</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.56">62A.56</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.46.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Long-term care policy.</span></h2>
          <p>"Long-term care policy" means an individual or group policy, certificate, subscriber contract, or other evidence of coverage that provides benefits for prescribed long-term care, including nursing facility services or home care services, or both nursing facility services and home care services, pursuant to the requirements of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.46">62A.46</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.56">62A.56</a>.</p>
          <p>Sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.46">62A.46</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.48">62A.48</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.52">62A.52</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.56">62A.56</a> do not apply to a long-term care policy issued to (a) an employer or employers or to the trustee of a fund established by an employer where only employees or retirees, and dependents of employees or retirees, are eligible for coverage or (b) to a labor union or similar employee organization. The associations exempted from the requirements of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.3099">62A.3099</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.44">62A.44</a> under <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.31#stat.62A.31.1">62A.31, subdivision 1</a>, clause (c) shall not be subject to the provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.46">62A.46</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.56">62A.56</a> until July 1, 1988.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.46.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Nursing facility.</span></h2>
          <p>"Nursing facility" means (1) a facility that is licensed as a nursing home under chapter 144A; (2) a facility that is both licensed as a boarding care home under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.50">144.50</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.56">144.56</a> and certified as an intermediate care facility for purposes of the medical assistance program; and (3) in states other than Minnesota, a facility that meets licensing and certification standards comparable to those that apply to the facilities described in clauses (1) and (2).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.46.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Home care services.</span></h2>
          <p>"Home care services" means one or more of the following prescribed services for the long-term care and treatment of an insured that are provided by a home health agency in a noninstitutional setting according to a written diagnosis or assessment and plan of care:</p>
          <p>(1) nursing and related personal care services under the direction of a registered nurse, including the services of a home health aide;</p>
          <p>(2) physical therapy;</p>
          <p>(3) speech therapy;</p>
          <p>(4) respiratory therapy;</p>
          <p>(5) occupational therapy;</p>
          <p>(6) nutritional services provided by a licensed dietitian;</p>
          <p>(7) homemaker services, meal preparation, and similar nonmedical services;</p>
          <p>(8) medical social services; and</p>
          <p>(9) other similar medical services and health-related support services.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.46.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Prescribed long-term care.</span></h2>
          <p>"Prescribed long-term care" means a service, type of care, or procedure that could not be omitted without adversely affecting the patient's illness or condition and is specified in a plan of care prepared by either: (1) a physician and a registered nurse and is appropriate and consistent with the diagnosis; or (2) a registered nurse or licensed social worker based on an assessment of the insured's ability to perform the activities of daily living and to perform basic cognitive functions appropriately.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.46.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Qualified insurer.</span></h2>
          <p>"Qualified insurer" means an entity licensed under chapter 62A or 62C.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.46.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Physician.</span></h2>
          <p>"Physician" means a medical practitioner licensed or holding a temporary permit under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/147.02">147.02</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/147.03">147.03</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/147.037">147.037</a>, or holding a residency permit under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/147.0391">147.0391</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.46.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Plan of care.</span></h2>
          <p>"Plan of care" means a written document prepared and signed by either: (1) a physician and registered nurse that specifies medically prescribed long-term care services or treatment that are consistent with the diagnosis; or (2) by a registered nurse or licensed social worker that specifies prescribed long-term care services or treatment that are consistent with an assessment of the insured's ability to perform the activities of daily living and to perform basic cognitive functions appropriately. The plan of care must be prepared in accordance with accepted standards of practice and must contain services or treatment that could not be omitted without adversely affecting the patient's illness or condition.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.46.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Insured.</span></h2>
          <p>"Insured" means a person covered under a long-term care policy.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.46.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Home health agency.</span></h2>
          <p>"Home health agency" means an entity that provides home care services and is (1) certified for participation in the Medicare program; or (2) licensed as a home health agency where a state licensing statute exists, or is otherwise acceptable to the insurer if licensing is not required.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.46.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Benefit period.</span></h2>
          <p>"Benefit period" means one or more separate or combined periods of confinement covered by a long-term care policy in a nursing facility or at home while receiving home care services. A benefit period begins on the first day the insured receives a benefit under the policy and ends when the insured has received no benefits for the same or related cause for an interval of 180 consecutive days.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.46.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Homebound or house confined.</span></h2>
          <p>"Homebound or house confined" means that a person is physically unable to leave the home without another person's aid because the person has lost the capacity of independent transportation or is disoriented.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.46.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Benefit day.</span></h2>
          <p>"Benefit day" means each day of confinement in a nursing facility or each visit for home care services. For purposes of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.48#stat.62A.48.1">62A.48, subdivision 1</a>, if the policyholder receives more than one home care service visit within a 24-hour period, each visit constitutes one benefit day.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.46.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/397/#laws.0.3.0">1986 c 397 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/337/#laws.0.58.0">1987 c 337 s 58</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/330/#laws.0.18.0">1989 c 330 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/551/#laws.0.1.0">1990 c 551 s 1</a>-4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/21/#laws.0.1.0">1993 c 21 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/258/#laws.0.31.0">1995 c 258 s 31</a>,32; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/446/#laws.1.36.0">1996 c 446 art 1 s 36</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/17/#laws.1.14.0">2005 c 17 art 1 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/40/#laws.1.7.0">2017 c 40 art 1 s 7</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.48">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.48 LONG-TERM CARE POLICIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.48.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Policy requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>No individual or group policy, certificate, subscriber contract, or other evidence of coverage of nursing home care or other long-term care services shall be offered, issued, delivered, or renewed in this state, whether or not the policy is issued in this state, unless the policy is offered, issued, delivered, or renewed by a qualified insurer and the policy satisfies the requirements of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.46">62A.46</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.56">62A.56</a>. A long-term care policy must cover prescribed long-term care in nursing facilities or the prescribed long-term home care services in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.46#stat.62A.46.4">62A.46, subdivision 4</a>, clauses (1) to (5), provided by a home health agency. A long-term care policy may cover both prescribed long-term care in nursing facilities and the prescribed long-term home care services in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.46#stat.62A.46.4">62A.46, subdivision 4</a>, clauses (1) to (5), provided by a home health agency. Coverage under a long-term care policy, other than one that covers only nursing facility services, must include: a minimum lifetime benefit limit of at least $25,000 for services. A long-term care policy that covers only nursing facility services must include a minimum lifetime benefit limit of not less than one year of nursing facility services. Nursing facility and home care coverages under a long-term care policy must not be subject to separate lifetime maximums for policies that cover both nursing facility and home health care. Prior hospitalization may not be required under a long-term care policy.</p>
          <p>The policy must cover preexisting conditions during the first six months of coverage if the insured was not diagnosed or treated for the particular condition during the 90 days immediately preceding the effective date of coverage. Coverage under the policy may include a waiting period of up to 180 days before benefits are paid, but there must be no more than one waiting period per benefit period; for purposes of this sentence, "days" can mean calendar or benefit days. If benefit days are used, an appropriate premium reduction and disclosure must be made. If benefit days are used in connection with coverage for home care services, the waiting period for home care services must not be longer than 90 benefit days. No policy may exclude coverage for mental or nervous disorders which have a demonstrable organic cause, such as Alzheimer's and related dementias. No policy may require the insured to be homebound or house confined to receive home care services. The policy must include a provision that the plan will not be canceled or renewal refused except on the grounds of nonpayment of the premium, provided that the insurer may change the premium rate on a class basis on any policy anniversary date. A provision that the policyholder may elect to have the premium paid in full at age 65 by payment of a higher premium up to age 65 may be offered. A provision that the premium would be waived during any period in which benefits are being paid to the insured during confinement in a nursing facility must be included. A nongroup policyholder may return a policy within 30 days of its delivery and have the premium refunded in full, less any benefits paid under the policy, if the policyholder is not satisfied for any reason.</p>
          <p>No individual long-term care policy shall be offered or delivered in this state until the insurer has received from the insured a written designation of at least one person, in addition to the insured, who is to receive notice of cancellation of the policy for nonpayment of premium. The insured has the right to designate up to a total of three persons who are to receive the notice of cancellation, in addition to the insured. The form used for the written designation must inform the insured that designation of one person is required and that designation of up to two additional persons is optional and must provide space clearly designated for listing between one and three persons. The designation shall include each person's full name, home address, and telephone number. Each time an individual policy is renewed or continued, the insurer shall notify the insured of the right to change this written designation.</p>
          <p>The insurer may file a policy form that utilizes a plan of care prepared as provided under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.46#stat.62A.46.5">62A.46, subdivision 5</a>, clause (1) or (2).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.48.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Per diem coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>If benefits are provided on a per diem basis, the minimum daily benefit for care in a nursing facility must be the lesser of $40 or actual charges under a long-term care policy and the minimum benefit per visit for home care under a long-term care policy must be the lesser of $25 or actual charges. The home care services benefit must cover at least seven paid visits per week.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.48.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Expense-incurred coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>If benefits are provided on an expense-incurred basis, a benefit of not less than 80 percent of covered charges for prescribed long-term care must be provided.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.48.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Loss ratio.</span></h2>
          <p>The anticipated loss ratio for long-term care policies must not be less than 65 percent for policies issued on a group basis or 60 percent for policies issued on an individual or mass-market basis. This subdivision does not apply to policies issued on or after January 1, 2002, that comply with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62S.021">62S.021</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62S.081">62S.081</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.48.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Solicitations by mail or media advertisement.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of this section, long-term care policies issued as a result of solicitations of individuals through mail or mass media advertising, including both print and broadcast advertising, shall be treated as individual policies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.48.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Coordination of benefits.</span></h2>
          <p>A long-term care policy may be secondary coverage for services provided under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.46">62A.46</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.56">62A.56</a>. Nothing in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.46">62A.46</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.56">62A.56</a> shall require the secondary payor to pay the obligations of the primary payor nor shall it prevent the secondary payor from recovering from the primary payor the amount of any obligation of the primary payor that the secondary payor elects to pay.</p>
          <p>There shall be no coordination of benefits between a long-term care policy and a policy designed primarily to provide coverage payable on a per diem, fixed indemnity or non-expense-incurred basis, or a policy that provides only accident coverage.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.48.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Existing policies.</span></h2>
          <p>Nothing in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.46">62A.46</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.56">62A.56</a> prohibits the renewal of the following long-term care policies:</p>
          <p>(1) policies sold outside the state of Minnesota to persons who at the time of sale were not residents of the state of Minnesota;</p>
          <p>(2) policies sold before August 1, 1986; and</p>
          <p>(3) policies sold before July 1, 1988, by associations exempted from sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.3099">62A.3099</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.44">62A.44</a> under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.31#stat.62A.31.6">62A.31, subdivision 6</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.48.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Cancellation for nonpayment of premium.</span></h2>
          <p>No individual long-term care policy shall be canceled for nonpayment of premium unless the insurer, at least 30 days before the effective date of the cancellation, has given notice to the insured and to those persons designated pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.48#stat.62A.48.1">62A.48, subdivision 1</a>, at the address provided by the insured for purposes of receiving notice of cancellation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.48.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Qualified long-term care.</span></h2>
          <p>Sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.46">62A.46</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.56">62A.56</a> do not apply to policies marketed as qualified long-term care insurance policies under chapter 62S.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.48.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Regulation of premiums and premium increases.</span></h2>
          <p>Policies issued under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.46">62A.46</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.56">62A.56</a> on or after January 1, 2002, must comply with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62S.021">62S.021</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62S.081">62S.081</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62S.265">62S.265</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62S.266">62S.266</a> to the same extent as policies issued under chapter 62S.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.48.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Nonforfeiture benefits.</span></h2>
          <p>Policies issued under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.46">62A.46</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.56">62A.56</a> on or after January 1, 2002, must comply with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62S.02#stat.62S.02.2">62S.02, subdivision 2</a>, to the same extent as policies issued under chapter 62S.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.48.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Regulatory flexibility.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may upon written request issue an order to modify or suspend a specific provision or provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.46">62A.46</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.56">62A.56</a> with respect to a specific long-term care insurance policy or certificate upon a written finding that:</p>
          <p>(1) the modification or suspension is in the best interest of the insureds;</p>
          <p>(2) the purpose to be achieved could not be effectively or efficiently achieved without the modifications or suspension; and</p>
          <p>(3)(i) the modification or suspension is necessary to the development of an innovative and reasonable approach for insuring long-term care;</p>
          <p>(ii) the policy or certificate is to be issued to residents of a life care or continuing care retirement community or some other residential community for the elderly and the modification or suspension is reasonably related to the special needs or nature of such a community; or</p>
          <p>(iii) the modification or suspension is necessary to permit long-term care insurance to be sold as part of, or in conjunction with, another insurance product.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.48.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/397/#laws.0.4.0">1986 c 397 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/337/#laws.0.59.0">1987 c 337 s 59</a>-62; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/689/#laws.2.8.0">1988 c 689 art 2 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/209/#laws.2.1.0">1989 c 209 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/330/#laws.0.19.0">1989 c 330 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/551/#laws.0.5.0">1990 c 551 s 5</a>-7; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/330/#laws.0.12.0">1993 c 330 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/625/#laws.8.4.0">1994 c 625 art 8 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/258/#laws.0.33.0">1995 c 258 s 33</a>,34; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/389/#laws.0.1.0">1996 c 389 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/446/#laws.1.37.0">1996 c 446 art 1 s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/71/#laws.2.5.0">1997 c 71 art 2 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/9/#laws.8.1.0">1Sp2001 c 9 art 8 s 1</a>-3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/379/#laws.1.113.0">2002 c 379 art 1 s 113</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/14/#laws.2.3.0">1Sp2003 c 14 art 2 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/17/#laws.1.14.0">2005 c 17 art 1 s 14</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.49">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.49 HOME CARE SERVICES COVERAGE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.49.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Generally.</span></h2>
          <p>Section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.48">62A.48</a> does not prohibit the sale of policies, certificates, subscriber contracts, or other evidences of coverage that provide home care services only. Home care services only policies may be sold, provided that they meet the requirements set forth in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.46">62A.46</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.56">62A.56</a>, except that they do not have to meet those conditions that relate to long-term care in nursing facilities. Disclosures and representations regarding these policies must be adjusted accordingly to remove references to coverage for nursing home care.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.49.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Provider networks and managed care.</span></h2>
          <p>Home health care services issued pursuant to this section may be provided through a limited provider network and may employ managed care practices. If these methods are used, they must be adequately disclosed within the policy and any advertisements or representations regarding coverage. Policies may not be sold in areas where there are not sufficient providers to meet the needs of the policyholders located in that area.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.49.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Prohibited limitations.</span></h2>
          <p>A long-term care insurance policy or certificate shall not, if it provides benefits for home health care or community care services, limit or exclude benefits by:</p>
          <p>(1) requiring that the insured would need care in a skilled nursing facility if home health care services were not provided;</p>
          <p>(2) requiring that the insured first or simultaneously receive nursing or therapeutic services in a home, community, or institutional setting before home health care services are covered;</p>
          <p>(3) limiting eligible services to services provided by a registered nurse or licensed practical nurse;</p>
          <p>(4) requiring that a nurse or therapist provide services covered by the policy that can be provided by a home health aide or other licensed or certified home care worker acting within the scope of licensure or certification;</p>
          <p>(5) excluding coverage for personal care services provided by a home health aide;</p>
          <p>(6) requiring that the provision of home health care services be at a level of certification or licensure greater than that required by the eligible service;</p>
          <p>(7) requiring that the insured have an acute condition before home health care services are covered;</p>
          <p>(8) limiting benefits to services provided by Medicare-certified agencies or providers;</p>
          <p>(9) excluding coverage for adult day care services; or</p>
          <p>(10) excluding coverage based upon location or type of residence in which the home health care services would be provided.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.49.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/485/#laws.0.32.0">1994 c 485 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/446/#laws.1.38.0">1996 c 446 art 1 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/14/#laws.2.4.0">1Sp2003 c 14 art 2 s 4</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.50">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.50 DISCLOSURES AND REPRESENTATIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.50.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Seal or emblems.</span></h2>
          <p>No graphic seal or emblem shall be displayed on any policy, or in connection with promotional materials on policy solicitations, that may reasonably be expected to convey to the purchaser that the policy form is approved, endorsed, or certified by a state or local unit of government or agency, the federal government, or a federal agency.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.50.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Cancellation notice.</span></h2>
          <p>Long-term care policies issued on a nongroup basis must have a notice prominently printed on the first page of the policy stating that the policyholder may return the policy within 30 days of its delivery and have the premium refunded in full if the policyholder is not satisfied for any reason. A solicitation for a long-term care policy to be issued on a nongroup basis pursuant to a direct-response solicitation must state in substance that the policyholder may return the policy within 30 days of its delivery and have the premium refunded in full if the policyholder is not satisfied for any reason.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.50.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Disclosures.</span></h2>
          <p>No long-term care policy shall be offered or delivered in this state, whether or not the policy is issued in this state, and no certificate of coverage under a group long-term care policy shall be offered or delivered in this state, unless a statement containing at least the following information is delivered to the applicant at the time the application is made:</p>
          <p>(1) a description of the benefits and coverage provided by the policy and the differences between this policy, a supplemental Medicare policy and the benefits to which an individual is entitled under parts A and B of Medicare;</p>
          <p>(2) a statement of the exceptions and limitations in the policy including the following language, as applicable, in bold print: "THIS POLICY DOES NOT COVER ALL NURSING CARE FACILITIES OR NURSING HOME, HOME CARE, OR ADULT DAY CARE EXPENSES AND DOES NOT COVER RESIDENTIAL CARE. READ YOUR POLICY CAREFULLY TO DETERMINE WHICH FACILITIES AND EXPENSES ARE COVERED BY YOUR POLICY.";</p>
          <p>(3) a statement of the renewal provisions including any reservation by the insurer of the right to change premiums;</p>
          <p>(4) a statement that the outline of coverage is a summary of the policy issued or applied for and that the policy should be consulted to determine governing contractual provisions;</p>
          <p>(5) an explanation of the policy's loss ratio including at least the following language: "This means that, on the average, policyholders may expect that $........ of every $100 in premium will be returned as benefits to policyholders over the life of the contract.";</p>
          <p>(6) a statement of the out-of-pocket expenses, including deductibles and co-payments for which the insured is responsible, and an explanation of the specific out-of-pocket expenses that may be accumulated toward any out-of-pocket maximum as specified in the policy;</p>
          <p>(7) the following language, in bold print: "YOUR PREMIUMS CAN BE INCREASED IN THE FUTURE. THE RATE SCHEDULE THAT LISTS YOUR PREMIUM NOW CAN CHANGE.";</p>
          <p>(8) the following language in bold print, with any provisions that are inapplicable to the particular policy omitted or crossed out: "THIS POLICY HAS A WAITING PERIOD OF ..... (CALENDAR OR BENEFIT) DAYS FOR NURSING CARE SERVICES AND A WAITING PERIOD OF ..... (CALENDAR OR BENEFIT) DAYS FOR HOME CARE SERVICES. THIS MEANS THAT THIS POLICY WILL NOT COVER YOUR CARE FOR THE FIRST ..... (CALENDAR OR BENEFIT) DAYS AFTER YOU ENTER A NURSING HOME, OR THE FIRST ..... (CALENDAR OR BENEFIT) DAYS AFTER YOU BEGIN TO USE HOME CARE SERVICES. YOU WOULD NEED TO PAY FOR YOUR CARE FROM OTHER SOURCES FOR THOSE WAITING PERIODS."; and</p>
          <p>(9) a signed and completed copy of the application for insurance is left with the applicant at the time the application is made.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.50.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Policies other than qualified long-term care insurance policies.</span></h2>
          <p>A policy that is not intended to be a qualified long-term care insurance policy as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62S.01#stat.62S.01.24">62S.01, subdivision 24</a>, must include a disclosure statement in the policy and in the outline of coverage that the policy is not intended to be a qualified long-term care insurance policy. The disclosure must be prominently displayed and read as follows: This long-term care insurance policy (certificate) is not intended to be a qualified long-term care insurance contract as defined under section 7702 (B)(b) of the Internal Revenue Code of 1986. You should consult with your attorney, accountant, or tax advisor regarding the tax implications of purchasing long-term care insurance.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.50.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/397/#laws.0.5.0">1986 c 397 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/337/#laws.0.63.0">1987 c 337 s 63</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/689/#laws.2.9.0">1988 c 689 art 2 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/258/#laws.0.35.0">1995 c 258 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/389/#laws.0.2.0">1996 c 389 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/71/#laws.2.6.0">1997 c 71 art 2 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/177/#laws.0.39.0">1999 c 177 s 39</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.52">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.52 REVIEW OF PLAN OF CARE.</h1>
        <p>The insurer may review an insured's plan of care at reasonable intervals, but not more frequently than once every 30 days.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.52.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/397/#laws.0.6.0">1986 c 397 s 6</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.54">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.54 PROHIBITED PRACTICES.</h1>
        <p>Unless otherwise provided for in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.46">62A.46</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.56">62A.56</a>, the solicitation or sale of long-term care policies is subject to the requirements and penalties applicable to the sale of Medicare supplement insurance policies as set forth in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.3099">62A.3099</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.44">62A.44</a>.</p>
        <p>It is misconduct for any agent or company to make any misstatements concerning eligibility or coverage under the medical assistance program, or about how long-term care costs will or will not be financed if a person does not have long-term care insurance. Any agent or company providing information on the medical assistance program shall also provide information about how to contact the county human services department or the state Department of Human Services.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.54.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/397/#laws.0.7.0">1986 c 397 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/689/#laws.2.10.0">1988 c 689 art 2 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/564/#laws.1.33.0">1992 c 564 art 1 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/17/#laws.1.14.0">2005 c 17 art 1 s 14</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.56">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.56 RULEMAKING.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.56.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Permissive.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may adopt rules pursuant to chapter 14 to carry out the purposes of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.46">62A.46</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.56">62A.56</a>. The rules may:</p>
          <p>(1) establish additional disclosure requirements for long-term care policies designed to adequately inform the prospective insured of the need and extent of coverage offered;</p>
          <p>(2) prescribe uniform policy forms in order to give the purchaser of long-term care policies a reasonable opportunity to compare the cost of insuring with various insurers; and</p>
          <p>(3) establish other reasonable minimum standards as needed to further the purposes of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.46">62A.46</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.56">62A.56</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.56.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Mandatory.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall adopt rules under chapter 14 establishing general standards to ensure that assessments used in the prescribing of long-term care are reliable, valid, and clinically appropriate.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.56.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/397/#laws.0.8.0">1986 c 397 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/551/#laws.0.8.0">1990 c 551 s 8</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.59">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.59 COVERAGE OF SERVICE; PRIOR AUTHORIZATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.59.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Service for which prior authorization not required.</span></h2>
          <p>A health carrier must not retrospectively deny or limit coverage of a health care service for which prior authorization was not required by the health carrier, unless there is evidence that the health care service was provided based on fraud or misinformation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.62A.59.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Service for which prior authorization required but not obtained.</span></h2>
          <p>A health carrier must not deny or limit coverage of a health care service which the enrollee has already received solely on the basis of lack of prior authorization if the service would otherwise have been covered had the prior authorization been obtained.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.62A.59.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.57.7.0">2024 c 127 art 57 s 7</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>This section, as added by Laws 2024, chapter 127, article 57, section 7, is effective January 1, 2026, and applies to health plans offered, sold, issued, or renewed on or after that date. Laws 2024, chapter 127, article 57, section 7, the effective date.</p>
      <h1 class="fgh">DENIAL OF EXPENSES</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.62A.60">
        <h1 class="shn">62A.60 RETROACTIVE DENIAL OF EXPENSES.</h1>
